		<Instruction showInTheGame = "False">
Welcome!   In this experiment, you will be playing a coordination game with some of the 
other players in the room.   Each person will have a slider on the screen that can be 
pushed either to the left or to the right.   To move the slider back and forth, use the 
arrow keys on the keyboard.  The goal of the game is for everybody to reach agreement on 
which side to push the slider.

On the screen, along with the slider, there is a number which shows percent  "Agreement".  
Each person is connected to some subset of the other people playing.  The Agreement 
percent value, shows how much you are agreeing with the rest of your group.  If everybody 
succeeds in agreeing with their group, then the whole community wins.    You can move your 
slider however you want to try the help your group reach agreement.   

NOTE:  Some configurations are not easy to win, but we are still interested in 
understanding how the process of coordination works, so please do your best!

Bottom line rules:  No talking while playing and don't look at other players' screens!

Please press "OK" when you're ready to begin.
